[u1132 | 01:03:58.932-01:04:00.732 | DM] seen as the instrument of the gods' will.

[u1142 | 01:04:40.252-01:04:43.112 | DM] If things go really off the rails, they can step in and be like,
[u1143 | 01:04:44.192-01:04:48.852 | DM] you know, appear as a giant head in the sky and be like, "No, this is wrong.
[u1144 | 01:04:49.692-01:04:51.352 | DM] He is not going to be your king."
[u1145 | 01:04:52.092-01:04:56.872 | DM] But that requires a huge amount of, of energy, and so they don't usually do that, right?
[u1146 | 01:04:56.872-01:05:01.672 | DM] 'Cause gods can't... It's very difficult for the gods to directly interact with the world.
[u1147 | 01:05:05.152-01:05:05.592 | DM] So

[u1150 | 01:05:13.212-01:05:13.511 | DM] you know...

[u3447 | 03:16:30.964-03:16:33.444 | DM] little sort of carved
[u3448 | 03:16:34.844-03:16:37.944 | DM] statues or mementos, sometimes out of wood or
[u3449 | 03:16:39.044-03:16:46.684 | DM] shaped out of clay or sometimes sculpted out of a sweet honey candy that people make for the festival.

[u3451 | 03:16:50.284-03:16:54.724 | DM] festival begins on this kind of solemn note and these morning prayers start.
[u3452 | 03:16:54.724-03:17:05.004 | DM] And that's what interrupts your conversation is hearing the beginning of these morning prayers echoing from the oasis, from the temple, just from, it's
[u3453 | 03:17:05.004-03:17:06.784 | DM] not really a formal religious thing.
[u3454 | 03:17:06.784-03:17:10.704 | DM] Like people aren't, it's not like there's a large crowd gathered at the
[u3455 | 03:17:11.724-03:17:15.704 | DM] temple of Shakun or at the temple of the five siblings in town either.
[u3456 | 03:17:16.404-03:17:21.304 | DM] It's more of family groups gathered together.
[u3457 | 03:17:21.304-03:17:25.784 | DM] And so you see people start to collect little campsites,
[u3458 | 03:17:27.304-03:17:29.284 | DM] these little dwellings that people make
[u3459 | 03:17:30.964-03:17:31.824 | DM] on the plains.
[u3460 | 03:17:33.424-03:17:44.384 | DM] People are getting up. You started, you were having your breakfast very early pre-dawn, but now people are getting up and the sounds of these prayers are starting to echo across
[u3461 | 03:17:44.384-03:17:48.924 | DM] the plains. And you can hear the drift of conversation as
[u3462 | 03:17:49.684-03:17:59.104 | DM] people will be going around as they're having their sort of sparse breakfast in this morning, telling stories, especially about those who have died in the past year.

[u3833 | 03:39:57.508-03:39:58.608 | DM] for Karawa, for,
[u3834 | 03:40:00.208-03:40:01.108 | DM] for all of us,
[u3835 | 03:40:02.028-03:40:04.828 | DM] all of the people here who live here, to protect them.
[u3836 | 03:40:08.868-03:40:09.628 | DM] And of course,
[u3837 | 03:40:12.728-03:40:13.628 | DM] you freed me.

[u3846 | 03:40:49.908-03:40:50.468 | DM] What say you?

[u3852 | 03:41:11.008-03:41:12.528 | DM] come with me west to Tokra,
[u3853 | 03:41:15.028-03:41:17.928 | DM] and beyond, depending on what the gods say.

[u4082 | 03:54:54.764-03:55:00.284 | DM] And he's like, "Hey, I heard from my cousin you were looking for me.

[u4093 | 03:55:36.664-03:55:44.384 | DM] I gave it to, to my cousin and he, and he, I guess he thought, uh, it looked like the sort of thing a, a, one of your people would enjoy.

[u4098 | 03:55:54.904-03:55:58.424 | Seeker] it has been of great use to me indeed, and a source of much learning.

[u4108 | 03:56:31.624-03:56:36.804 | DM] I, I'm not, not sure I remember exactly.

[u4111 | 03:56:44.104-03:56:45.144 | DM] ... not, not too far out.

[u4178 | 04:01:02.448-04:01:07.128 | Wellby] We can promise not to pass this information on to anyone else,

[u4190 | 04:01:44.388-04:01:51.868 | Kenzo] So, so we can ask him something in a way that will make him say some stuff, and then we can figure out what about it is false or true.

[u4192 | 04:01:55.188-04:01:55.248 | DM] Okay.
[u4193 | 04:01:55.248-04:01:56.128 | Unknown] I will Sense the Truth.

[u4219 | 04:03:10.788-04:03:12.808 | DM] and there's plenty of people who buy those.

[u4222 | 04:03:23.728-04:03:24.008 | DM] It's...

[u4225 | 04:03:35.188-04:03:36.648 | DM] but everybody's gotta eat, right?
[u4226 | 04:03:39.688-04:03:42.848 | DM] So I've been going out there every now and then.

[u4228 | 04:03:47.188-04:03:51.948 | DM] It's pretty dangerous to go too far past Basuda, as you get into the real wildlands.
[u4229 | 04:03:54.248-04:03:57.208 | DM] But if you go only a few days, sometimes you find some good stuff.

[u4241 | 04:04:49.660-04:04:51.800 | DM] and everything was looted but these books.
[u4242 | 04:04:51.800-04:04:56.380 | DM] [coughs] What was particularly strange about this
[u4243 | 04:04:57.860-04:04:58.220 | DM] is I...

[u4248 | 04:05:31.100-04:05:35.200 | DM] It's like the pouch had exploded and spewed out books.

[u4251 | 04:05:40.400-04:05:41.440 | Unknown] Bag of holding.

[u4264 | 04:06:15.320-04:06:20.180 | DM] professor or something s- told me he, he worked at a library,

[u4284 | 04:07:23.200-04:07:23.400 | DM] uh,
[u4285 | 04:07:26.040-04:07:29.140 | DM] gray eyes, wrinkled skin.
[u4286 | 04:07:29.140-04:07:31.560 | DM] Had, had all his teeth.
[u4287 | 04:07:33.040-04:07:36.580 | DM] Seemed pretty healthy and spry for his, for how old he was.
[u4288 | 04:07:42.720-04:07:43.120 | DM] But, uh,
[u4289 | 04:07:44.460-04:07:46.380 | DM] yeah, he was very excited to find these books.
[u4290 | 04:07:47.840-04:07:48.360 | DM] Said he was
[u4291 | 04:07:50.100-04:07:52.780 | DM] collecting records of history
[u4292 | 04:07:54.660-04:07:56.620 | DM] for some great library.
[u4293 | 04:07:57.920-04:08:00.580 | DM] It's like, "Whatever, dude, just give me the money.

[u4540 | 04:22:37.500-04:22:42.080 | Seeker] Perhaps the times ahead will hold a closer alliance between the dwarves and the Stoneborn.
